Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The petrochemical variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 963 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 55 and intersection form of rank 1.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
